rr-mask">We are seeking a highly experienced developer to maintain OwnerFinancing.ai, an existing real estate marketplace. This is an ongoing monthly engagement for a dependable professional who can take responsibility for keeping the website stable, secure, fast, and functioning correctly.

We want a developer who can diagnose problems, implement reliable fixes, and communicate clearly with the owner.

Monthly Responsibilities

Monitor website availability, application errors, and performance.

Diagnose and fix frontend, backend, database, and integration issues.

Maintain existing registration, login, property listing, search, messaging, and subscription functionality.

Resolve mobile usability problems and broken pages, links, images, or forms.

Maintain dependencies and address relevant security updates.

Monitor scheduled jobs, email delivery, and existing third-party integrations.

Maintain backups and periodically verify that restoration works.

Preserve technical SEO essentials, including metadata, redirects, canonical URLs, and sitemap functionality.

Test changes before deployment and maintain a reliable rollback process.

Provide a monthly report covering completed work, outstanding issues, hours used, and recommended priorities.

Required Experience

Strong experience maintaining live, database-driven web applications.

Ability to work confidently across frontend, backend, database, and hosting environments.

Excellent debugging and troubleshooting skills.

Experience with authentication, payments, APIs, and production deployments.

Strong understanding of application security and performance.

Clear written communication and dependable availability.

Please review the existing codebase and confirm the technology stack before finalizing your maintenance proposal. An existing website audit will be available to help establish priorities.

Working Arrangement

We prefer a monthly retainer with clearly defined included hours, responsibilities, and response times. Please specify emergency support availability and the rate for work beyond the monthly allowance.

Additional feature development will be agreed and priced separately. All code, accounts, documentation, and project assets must remain under our ownership.

Include in Your Application

Two or three relevant websites you have personally maintained, explaining your responsibilities.

An example of a difficult production issue you diagnosed and resolved.

Your proposed monthly fee, included hours, and additional hourly rate.

Your working hours, time zone, and response time for critical issues.

Your approach to monitoring, backups, testing, and deployments.

Whether you will personally perform the work.

Your earliest availability.

We are looking for a long-term technical partner with proven production experience. JobDescription"><!----><div id="air3-line-clamp-13" class="is-clamped air3-line-clamp" tabindex="-1"><!--[--><p class="mb-0 text-body-sm rr-mask"># Job Title: Manual QA Tester – Mobile App &amp; Admin Panel (Remote)

We are looking for a detail-oriented **Manual QA Tester** to perform comprehensive functional testing on our mobile application and web-based admin panel.

### Responsibilities
- Perform functional testing on the mobile app (iOS &amp; Android).
- Test all features and workflows in the Admin Panel.
- Identify bugs, errors, and unexpected behaviors.
- Report bugs with clear reproduction steps, screenshots, and screen recordings.
- Perform regression testing after bug fixes.
- Verify that all features work according to the requirements.
- Test user roles, permissions, forms, validations, and business workflows.
- Maintain organized testing reports and track unresolved issues.

### Requirements
- Proven experience in manual QA and functional testing.
- Experience testing mobile applications and web-based admin panels.
- Strong attention to detail and ability to identify functional issues.
- Ability to write clear, structured bug reports.
- Familiarity with Jira or similar bug-tracking tools.
- Ability to work independently.

### Salary &amp; Employment
- **Monthly Salary: $250 USD (Fixed – Non-Negotiable)**
- Remote position.
- Long-term collaboration.
- Continuous testing as new features are developed.

### Screening Questions
1. Have you read the job description carefully, and do you agree to the fixed monthly salary of $250?
2. How many years of experience do you have in manual QA testing?
3. Have you tested both mobile applications and web-based admin panels?
4. What tools do you use for bug reporting and test case management?
5. Can you create detailed test cases and independently test complete workflows?

**Important:** We are looking for someone who can thoroughly test the entire system, identify issues proactively, and provide clear, actionable bug reports.</p><!--]--></div><!----></div><div determined</strong></li></ul><div class="air3-line-clamp-wrapper clamp mb-3" style="--lines:2;" data-ev-sublocation="!line_clamp" data-test="UpCLineClamp JobDescription"><!----><div id="air3-line-clamp-28" class="is-clamped air3-line-clamp" tabindex="-1"><!--[--><p class="mb-0 text-body-sm rr-mask">ABOUT US

We have recently launched a new B2B technology website:

https://itpartnerhq.com

IT Partner is a Microsoft-focused cloud and technology company working with business customers internationally. Our services include Microsoft 365, Azure, Microsoft Copilot, Security, Microsoft licensing, cloud migrations, and related professional services.

We are now investing in SEO, link building, content, and lead generation.

Before scaling these activities further, we are looking for an experienced B2B Website QA, UX &amp; CRO Specialist to perform a comprehensive independent review of our website and potentially continue working with us on a part-time basis.

This is NOT simply a website bug-testing assignment.

We need someone who can combine technical QA, UX/usability and Conversion Rate Optimization (CRO).

The main question we want you to answer is:

"If I were a potential B2B customer, what could prevent me from understanding the company's offering, trusting the company, finding what I need, and eventually requesting a quote or contacting the sales team?"

WHAT YOU WILL REVIEW

WEBSITE QA

Please test the website for:

Broken links
Navigation and menu issues
Buttons and CTAs
Contact and quote-request forms
Form validation and error handling
Confirmation/success messages
404 pages and redirects
Layout/display problems
Image issues
Mobile responsiveness
Cross-browser compatibility
Basic accessibility/usability issues
Page-loading and rendering issues

Testing should ideally cover current versions of Chrome, Edge, Safari and Firefox across desktop and mobile.

UX &amp; USABILITY

Evaluate the site as a real first-time business customer.

We want to understand:

Is it immediately clear what IT Partner does?
Is our value proposition clear?
Does the business appear credible and trustworthy?
Is navigation intuitive?
Is it easy to find relevant Microsoft products and services?
Are pages easy to understand and scan?
Is there a logical customer journey?
Does every important commercial page have a clear next step?
Are there unnecessary distractions or friction?
Is the mobile experience strong?
Would a potential business customer feel comfortable contacting us?
CONVERSION RATE OPTIMIZATION

Review our ability to convert relevant visitors into qualified enquiries.

Please assess:

CTA wording
CTA placement
Above-the-fold messaging
Landing-page structure
Value propositions
Request-a-quote journeys
Contact forms
Form friction
Lead qualification
Trust signals
Social proof
Objection handling
Mobile conversion
Missing conversion opportunities

We do not necessarily want the same CTA everywhere.

Depending on the customer's intent, examples could include:

Request a Quote
Request Microsoft 365 Pricing
Talk to a Microsoft Specialist
Discuss Your Azure Project
Request a Migration Assessment
Discuss Copilot Readiness

We want your professional recommendations based on visitor intent.

CUSTOMER JOURNEY TESTING

Please test several realistic B2B journeys, for example:

A company searching for Microsoft 365 licensing and pricing.

A company considering Microsoft 365 Copilot and needing licensing/readiness assistance.

A company looking for an Azure partner.

A company planning a Microsoft 365/cloud migration.

For each scenario, please evaluate the journey:

Find - Understand - Trust - Take Action - Submit Enquiry

Identify where the customer may become confused, lose trust, encounter friction, abandon the website, or fail to take the next step.

TOOLS

Experience with tools such as the following is useful:

Microsoft Clarity
Google Analytics 4
Google Search Console
Google Lighthouse
PageSpeed Insights
Screaming Frog
BrowserStack
Chrome DevTools
Hotjar

You do not need to use every tool. We care more about your analysis and professional judgment.

DELIVERABLES

We do NOT want an automatically generated website audit.

For significant findings, please provide:

Page/URL
Description of the issue
Screenshot where appropriate
Why it matters
Recommended solution
Priority

Please prioritize findings as:

Critical High Medium Low

We would also like:

A prioritized QA/UX/CRO audit
Quick wins
CRO recommendations
Customer journey recommendations
Larger strategic recommendations
20-45 minute Loom or similar video walkthrough of the main findings

ONGOING PART-TIME WORK

We want to start with the initial comprehensive audit.

If we are happy with the cooperation, we would like to keep the right person involved on a part-time/project basis for:

Website QA
Testing new pages and functionality
UX reviews
CRO analysis
Landing-page optimization
Form optimization
Customer journey analysis
Microsoft Clarity analysis
A/B testing
Ongoing conversion improvements

We are looking for a specialist with whom we can potentially build a long-term working relationship.

WHO SHOULD APPLY

We are particularly interested in candidates with experience in:

B2B website QA
Conversion Rate Optimization
UX/usability
SaaS
IT services
Cloud services
Cybersecurity
MSPs
Enterprise technology
Lead-generation websites

Microsoft-specific experience is welcome but not required.

Strong B2B CRO/UX experience is more important to us.

HOW TO APPLY

Please start your proposal with:

QA + CRO

Then answer the following:

Please provide 2-3 examples of B2B websites you have personally audited or optimized.

What important QA, UX or CRO problems did you identify?

What improvements did you recommend, and what results did they produce if measurable?

Which tools do you normally use?

Have you worked with SaaS, IT, cloud or other complex B2B businesses?

Please spend a few minutes reviewing https://itpartnerhq.com and tell us the first 3 things you would investigate or potentially improve, and WHY.

What would you charge for the initial comprehensive audit?

How long would the audit take?

What is your hourly rate and weekly availability for potential ongoing work?

Please do not send a generic proposal or an automatically generated website audit.

We are more interested in how you think about our actual website, our customers, and conversion opportunities.</p><!--]--></div><!----></div><div class="air3-token-container" data-v-6c5a9165="" data-test="TokenClamp JobAttrs" data-test-key="2"><!----><!--[--><div class="air3-token-wrap" data-v-6c5a9165=""><button class="air3-token" data-test="token" type="button" data-v-6c5a9165=""><span
